$Event(13000200, Default, function() {
    if (!ThisEventSlot()) {
        WaitFor(!InArea(10000, 3002550));
        SetEventFlag(13000201, ON);
        EndEvent();
    }
L0:
    SetEventFlag(13000201, ON);
});

    EndIf(EventFlag(13000009));
    WaitFor(EventFlag(13000009));
    SetEventFlag(13000000, OFF);
});

$Event(13000230, Default, function() {
    EndIf(ThisEventSlot());
    SetVisibilityOfMessage(3002280, Disabled);
    WaitFor(PlayerHasItemIncludingBBox(ItemType.Goods, 2117));
    SetVisibilityOfMessage(3002280, Enabled);
});

$Event(13000360, Default, function() {
    EndIf(PlayerIsNotInOwnWorld());
    SetNetworkSyncState(Disabled);
    EndIf(ThisEventSlot());
    WaitFor(EventFlag(711));
    ForceAnimationPlayback(10000, 63010, false, false, false, 0, 1);
    SpawnOneshotSFX(TargetEntityType.Character, 10000, 245, 30300);
    CreateObjectfollowingSFX(3001705, 90, 830095);
    SetEventFlag(13000360, ON);
    WaitFixedTimeSeconds(10);
    DeleteObjectfollowingSFX(3001705, true);
});

$Event(13000380, Default, function(chrEntityId, itemLotId, eventFlagId) {
    EndIf(PlayerIsNotInOwnWorld());
    EndIf(ThisEventSlot() && EventFlag(eventFlagId));
    WaitFor(CharacterDead(chrEntityId));
    AwardItemsIncludingClients(itemLotId);
    EndEvent();
});

$Event(13000410, Default, function() {
    EndIf(ThisEventSlot());
    SetEventFlag(13000300, ON);
    SetEventFlag(13000310, ON);
    EndEvent();
});

$Event(13105210, Restart, function(eventFlagId, entityId, objEntityId, objactEventFlag, objEntityId2, objactEventFlag2, objEntityId3, objactEventFlag3) {
    if (!EventFlag(eventFlagId)) {
        SetObjactState(objEntityId2, -1, Enabled);
        SetObjactState(objEntityId, -1, Disabled);
        SetObjactState(objEntityId3, -1, Disabled);
        obj = ObjActEventFlag(objactEventFlag2);
        obj2 = ObjActEventFlag(objactEventFlag3);
        WaitFor(
            obj
                || obj2
                || EventFlag(eventFlagId)
                || InArea(10000, 3102201)
                || InArea(10000, 3102202));
        SetObjactState(objEntityId2, -1, Disabled);
        SetObjactState(objEntityId3, -1, Disabled);
        GotoIf(L0, obj.Passed);
        GotoIf(L1, obj2.Passed);
        ForceAnimationPlayback(entityId, 21, false, true, true, 0, 1);
        Goto(L2);
L0:
        WaitFixedTimeSeconds(2);
        ForceAnimationPlayback(entityId, 21, false, true, true, 0, 1);
        ForceAnimationPlayback(objEntityId2, 3, false, false, true, 0, 1);
        Goto(L2);
L1:
        WaitFixedTimeSeconds(2);
        ForceAnimationPlayback(entityId, 21, false, true, true, 0, 1);
        ForceAnimationPlayback(objEntityId3, 3, false, false, true, 0, 1);
        Goto(L2);
L2:
        WaitFor(!AllPlayersInArea(3102202) && !AllPlayersInArea(3102203));
        ForceAnimationPlayback(entityId, 110, false, true, true, 0, 1);
        ForceAnimationPlayback(entityId, 10, false, true, true, 0, 1);
        SetEventFlag(eventFlagId, ON);
        SetObjactState(objEntityId, -1, Enabled);
        SetObjactState(objEntityId3, -1, Disabled);
        RestartEvent();
    }
L3:
    SetObjactState(objEntityId, -1, Enabled);
    SetObjactState(objEntityId2, -1, Disabled);
    SetObjactState(objEntityId3, -1, Disabled);
    obj3 = ObjActEventFlag(objactEventFlag);
    obj4 = ObjActEventFlag(objactEventFlag3);
    WaitFor(
        obj3 || obj4 || !EventFlag(eventFlagId) || InArea(10000, 3102202) || InArea(10000, 3102203));
    SetObjactState(objEntityId, -1, Disabled);
    SetObjactState(objEntityId3, -1, Disabled);
    GotoIf(L4, obj3.Passed);
    GotoIf(L5, obj4.Passed);
    ForceAnimationPlayback(entityId, 12, false, true, true, 0, 1);
    Goto(L6);
L4:

    WaitFor(
        (((CharacterType(10000, TargetType.BlackPhantom) && CharacterHasSpEffect(10000, 3710))
            || CharacterType(10000, TargetType.Alive)
            || CharacterType(10000, TargetType.Hollow)
            || CharacterType(10000, TargetType.WhitePhantom))
            && (EntityInRadiusOfEntity(10000, chrEntityId, targetDistance, 1)
                || InArea(10000, areaEntityId)))
            || CharacterDamagedBy(chrEntityId, 10000));
    EndIf(!CharacterHasSpEffect(chrEntityId, 5450));
    if (!EntityInRadiusOfEntity(10000, chrEntityId, targetDistance, 1)) {
        WaitFixedTimeSeconds(timeSeconds);
    }
    EndIf(!CharacterHasSpEffect(chrEntityId, 5450));
    ForceAnimationPlayback(chrEntityId, animationId2, false, false, false, 0, 1);
    ClearSpEffect(chrEntityId, 12091);
    RequestCharacterAIReplan(chrEntityId);
    EndEvent();
});

$Event(130, Default, function(areaId, blockId, hitEntityId, ceremonyId, achievementId) {
    EndIf(ThisEventSlot());
    mapHit &= PlayerInMap(areaId, blockId) && PlayerStandingOnHit(hitEntityId);
    if (PlayerInMap(40, 0)) {
        mapHit &= MapCeremony(areaId, blockId, ceremonyId);
    }
    WaitFor(mapHit);
    EndIf(achievementId == -1);
    AwardAchievement(achievementId);
    EndEvent();
});

$Event(20006031, Default, function(eventFlagId, areaEntityId) {
    EndIf(PlayerIsNotInOwnWorld());
    SetEventFlag(eventFlagId, OFF);
    WaitFor(InArea(10000, areaEntityId));
    SetEventFlag(eventFlagId, ON);
    WaitFixedTimeSeconds(1);
    WaitFor(!InArea(10000, areaEntityId));
    SetEventFlag(eventFlagId, OFF);
    RestartEvent();
});

$Event(20006032, Default, function(chrEntityId, objEntityId) {
    EndIf(PlayerIsNotInOwnWorld());
    if (HPRatio(chrEntityId) != 0) {
        WaitFor(CharacterDead(chrEntityId));
    }
L0:
    WarpObjectToCharacter(objEntityId, chrEntityId, 236);
    WaitFor(
        (!EventFlag(73501050)
            || !EventFlag(73501010)
            || !EventFlag(73501020)
            || !EventFlag(73501030)
            || !EventFlag(73501040))
            && EventFlag(50006202));
    CreateObjectfollowingSFX(objEntityId, 90, 61);
    WaitFor(ActionButtonInArea(4000, objEntityId));
    ForceAnimationPlayback(10000, 60070, false, false, false, 0, 1);
    AwardItemLot(52020);
    DeleteObjectfollowingSFX(objEntityId, true);
});

$Event(20006040, Default, function(chrEntityId, entityId, spEffectId) {
    WaitFor(
        !PlayerIsNotInOwnWorld()
            && CharacterBackreadStatus(chrEntityId)
            && CharacterHasSpEffect(chrEntityId, spEffectId));
    WarpCharacterAndCopyFloor(chrEntityId, TargetEntityType.Area, entityId, -1, chrEntityId);
    WaitFor(!PlayerIsNotInOwnWorld() && !CharacterBackreadStatus(chrEntityId));
    RestartEvent();
});
